<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于盐结晶诱导流动的研究所报道的流速数据可能因表面污染而被低估，需要澄清液滴清洁度对结晶流动机制的影响。

Method: 通过实验测量洁净条件下氯化钠在静态液滴中结晶时的流动速度，并结合数值模拟对比清洁与受污染两种情况下的流动驱动机制。

Result: 测得结晶过程中流速约为1 cm/s，是此前研究报道值的两个数量级；清洁条件下流动由Marangoni应力驱动，而受污染条件下由密度差驱动。

Conclusion: 液滴表面清洁度是决定结晶诱导流动性质的关键因素，进而影响晶体的生长速率。

Abstract: In this article, we study the flow induced by crystallization of sodium chloride in sessile droplets. We measure flow velocities on the order of 1 $\mathrm{cm.s^{-1}} during crystallization, two orders of magnitude higher than those reported in previous studies. We show that this discrepancy is due to surface contaminants in earlier experiments. Numerical simulations reveal that in the clean case, the flow is driven by Marangoni stresses, whereas it is density- driven in the contaminated case. Consequently, droplet cleanliness significantly influences the flow which, in turn, determines the growth rate of crystals.

</details>


### [88] [Propeller-wing interactions of a lift + cruise eVTOL configuration during transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07665)
*Oliver Westcott,Swathi Krishna,Robert Entwistle*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究通过风洞试验探究了升降+巡航飞行器垂直升降螺旋桨与机翼之间的轴向和纵向间距变化对其从悬停到前飞过渡阶段气动性能的影响。结果表明过渡早期阶段（前螺旋桨前进比0至0.25）对构型变化最为敏感，后电机取向和纵向间距对升阻比有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 升降+巡航飞行器在从悬停过渡到前飞的关键阶段，其垂直升降螺旋桨的尾流会与机翼相互作用，而这种相互作用对该飞行器构型的气动性能影响尚不清楚，特别是螺旋桨与机翼之间的几何配置变化如何影响这一过渡过程。

Method: 通过在两个纵向位置以直立或倒置方式改变电机取向引入轴向变化，对四种升降+巡航机翼构型进行风洞试验，雷诺数范围52,000至377,000，利用流场显示丝线、压力传感器和负载传感器测量气动特性。

Result: 发现前螺旋桨前进比0至0.25的早期过渡阶段对构型变化最为敏感。在最短纵向间距下，后电机直立取向时主动升降螺旋桨使设计升阻比提高约4个单位，而后电机倒置时升阻比下降4个点。减小纵向间距放大了后螺旋桨的有利升力贡献和前螺旋桨的不利影响，但在直立取向下将前螺旋桨靠近机翼在前进比0.11至0.25区间产生了有利的螺旋桨尾涡与机翼相互作用从而提升了性能。

Conclusion: 过渡早期阶段的气动性能强烈依赖于升降螺旋桨与机翼的几何构型，电机取向和纵向间距是关键设计参数；合理的配置可以显著提升升阻比，且前螺旋桨与机翼间存在可利用的有利尾涡交互机制。

Abstract: The wakes of a lift + cruise aircraft's vertical lift propellers interact with the wing during the transition from hover to forward flight. This study investigates how variations in axial and longitudinal separations between the vertical lift propellers and the wing affect aerodynamic performance during this critical phase. Axial variations were introduced by orienting the motors either upright or inverted at two longitudinal positions relative to the wing. Wind tunnel tests were conducted on the resulting lift + cruise wing configurations over a Reynolds number range of 52,000 to 377,000, using tufts, pressure taps, and load cells to capture aerodynamic behaviour. Results show that the early transition phase (front propeller advance ratios between 0 and 0.25) is particularly sensitive to configuration changes. Rear motor orientation had a pronounced effect: when oriented upright at the shortest longitudinal separation, the active lift propellers increased the lift-drag ratio of the design by approximately 4 units, whereas in the rear motor inverted case the design suffered a 4-point reduction in the lift-drag ratio. Decreasing the longitudinal separation amplified both the beneficial lift contribution of the rear propeller and the generally adverse effects of the front propeller. However, placing the front propeller closer to the wing in the upright orientation enhanced performance at advance ratios between 0.11 and 0.25, due to favourable interactions between the wing and the front propeller's trailing vortices.

</details>


### [89] [Coupled thermoacoustic resolvent analysis of a model two-stream coaxial combustor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08096)
*Lu Chen,Zheng Qiao,Yu Lv*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文推导了一个求解算子（resolvent operator）来分析双流同轴燃烧室中的火焰-声学耦合效应。该理论同时考虑了活性火焰的水动力学效应、燃烧室声学效应及二者的耦合。将该工具应用于双流同轴模型燃烧室后发现，耦合系统相比纯水动力线性系统呈现截然不同的强迫-响应特性，包括共振声能峰值、声学强迫不满足秩一性质，并通过新颖的灵敏度分析识别出对火焰-声学耦合效应最敏感的模态。此外，利用耦合热声求解算子可设计强迫来抵消另一属性的扰动，有助于发展热声控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析方法通常仅关注火焰的水动力学不稳定性或燃烧室的声学响应，无法全面刻画火焰与声学之间的耦合作用。理解这种耦合下的最优强迫与响应特性，对于揭示燃烧不稳定机理和开发有效控制策略具有重要意义。

Method: 推导了一个耦合热声求解算子（coupled thermoacoustic resolvent），同时纳入火焰的水动力效应、燃烧室的声学效应及其耦合作用。利用该算子识别不同机制的最优强迫与最优响应，并引入新颖的灵敏度分析方法识别对火焰-声学耦合最敏感的模态。

Result: 耦合系统表现出与纯水动力线性系统显著不同的强迫-响应特性：观测到指示共振的声能峰值；声学强迫不保持秩一（rank-one）性质；识别出对火焰-声学耦合效应最敏感的模态。此外，通过耦合热声求解算子证明，可设计强迫来抵消另一属性强迫引起的扰动。

Conclusion: 所提出的耦合热声求解算子为分析火焰-声学耦合效应提供了一套有效框架，不仅揭示了耦合系统的独特动力学特征，还为热声不稳定性的被动/主动控制策略提供了新的理论依据和实现途径。

Abstract: We derived a resolvent operator to analyze the coupled flame-acoustic effect in a model two-stream coaxial combustor. The theoretical analysis accommodates both the hydrodynamic effect of the active flame and chamber acoustic effect, as well as their coupling effect. Utilizing the coupled thermoacoustic resolvent, we are able to identify the optimal forcing of different mechanisms and their corresponding optimal responses. When this new modeling tool is applied to a two-stream coaxial model combustor, it reveals distinctly different forcing-response characteristics in the coupled system compared to those in a purely hydrodynamic linear system. Acoustic energy peaks, indicative of resonance, are observed. Additionally, it has been found that the acoustic forcing does not maintain a rank-one property. Furthermore, the modes most receptive to the flame-acoustic coupling effect can be identified using a novel sensitivity analysis approach. Interestingly, by employing the coupled thermoacoustic resolvent, we demonstrate that forcing can be designed to negate or counteract perturbations caused by the forcing of another attribute, potentially aiding in the development of effective thermoacoustic control strategies.

</details>


### [90] [Closed-Form Solution for Oscillatory Flow and Wall Shear Stress in Axisymmetric Corrugated Tubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08651)
*Frederik Keil,Paolo Malgaretti,Othmane Aouane,Jens Harting*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Womersley经典解，利用润滑近似推导了缓变半径刚性轴对称管中振荡流的闭式表达式，包括轴向速度剖面、流量、相位滞后和壁面剪应力，并采用三维格子玻尔兹曼模拟验证了理论的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波纹管中的脉动流广泛存在于血流动力学和微流控中，其中振荡驱动力与几何收缩共同决定输运和壁面载荷。经典Womersley解仅适用于刚性圆形直管，缺乏对缓慢变径管中振荡流的解析描述，因此有必要开发一个扩展理论来分析复杂几何下的脉动流行为。

Method: 在润滑近似框架下，将Womersley振荡流解扩展至刚性轴对称缓变管，导出任意Womersley数下轴向速度剖面、体积流量、相位滞后和壁面剪应力的闭式表达式；同时用三维格子玻尔兹曼模拟（LBM）评估理论有效范围。

Result: 分析表明局部速度剖面沿管变化显著，宽段接近plug状、窄段接近抛物线形；循环最大流量随波纹度增大而减小，但其减弱程度随脉动增加而减轻，表现出从准稳态尺度R^-4平均到高频尺度R^-2平均的交叉；瓶颈处壁面剪应力最大且随Womersley数增大而减小；对于正弦波纹，瓶颈壁面剪应力与波纹度呈非单调关系（局部剪切放大与全局水力阻力竞争）。文中还给出了时间平均壁面剪应力和振荡剪切指数的闭式表达式。

Conclusion: 该理论将标准振荡流解析框架推广到几何变径管场景，提供了对波纹管脉动流输运与壁面载荷的定量描述，并与标准血流动力学指标建立了联系，对理解真实血管与微流控通道中的脉动流具有参考价值。

Abstract: Pulsatile flow in corrugated tubes arises in hemodynamics and microfluidics, where oscillatory forcing and geometric constrictions jointly determine transport and wall loading. We extend Womersley's classical solution for oscillatory flow in a rigid circular tube to rigid axisymmetric tubes of slowly varying radius. Within the lubrication approximation, we derive closed-form expressions for the axial velocity profile, volumetric flow rate, phase lag, and wall shear stress at an arbitrary Womersley number. Three-dimensional lattice Boltzmann simulations are used to assess the regime of validity of the theory. The analysis shows that the local velocity profile varies strongly along the tube, ranging from plug-like in wide sections to more parabolic near bottlenecks. The cycle-maximum flow rate decreases with increasing corrugation, but this reduction weakens as pulsatility increases, reflecting a crossover from the quasi-steady scaling $\langle R^{-4}\rangle^{-1}$ to the high-frequency scaling $\langle R^{-2}\rangle^{-1}$. The wall shear stress is maximal at the bottleneck and decreases with Womersley number. For sinusoidal corrugations, the bottleneck wall shear stress depends non-monotonically on the corrugation because of the competition between local shear amplification and global hydraulic resistance. Closed-form expressions for the time-averaged wall shear stress and oscillatory shear index further connect the theory to standard hemodynamic metrics.

</details>


### [91] [ONE CYLinder: A Benchmark for Graph-Based Surrogate Modeling of Unsteady Bluff-Body Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08947)
*Théodore Michel,Antoine Campos,Alban Dujardin,Henry Areiza,Philippe Meliga,Elie Hachem*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文介绍ONECYL基准数据集及其配套的图Transformer基线模型，用于非结构网格上非定常圆柱绕流的长时间自回归预测，涵盖层流、过渡流和高雷诺数三种流态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的代理模型有望加速非结构网格上的CFD仿真，但缺乏覆盖多种流态的基准数据集和标准化的长时间自回归预测评估协议，限制了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建ONECYL基准：包含450个高保真变分多尺度有限元仿真（270,000个快照），随机化圆柱几何参数，覆盖层流、过渡流和高雷诺数三种流态；提供速度/压力场、网格连通性、几何描述符、雷诺数及空气动力学积分量。提出统一的评估框架，结合全场rollout误差、虚拟探针、阻力与升力预测。开发图Transformer作为参考基线，在非结构网格上自回归预测速度与压力场，并研究level-set几何表示和基于散度的物理正则化。

Result: 显式level-set几何编码一致地提升了长时间预测精度及对未见几何的泛化能力；基于散度的正则化在流复杂度增加时愈发有效。

Conclusion: ONECYL及其图Transformer基线为图代理模型提供了可复现的评估框架，为非定常钝体流长时间预测的未来研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Graph-based surrogate models offer a promising route to accelerate computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations on unstructured meshes. However, their development is limited by the scarcity of benchmark datasets spanning multiple flow regimes and standardized protocols for long-horizon autoregressive prediction. We introduce ONECYL (ONE CYLinder), a new benchmark for unsteady flow past a circular cylinder across laminar, transitional, and high-Reynolds-number regimes. The benchmark comprises 450 high-fidelity Variational Multiscale finite-element simulations (270,000 flow snapshots) with randomized cylinder geometries, providing time-resolved velocity and pressure fields together with mesh connectivity, geometric descriptors, Reynolds numbers, and integrated aerodynamic quantities. Beyond the dataset, ONECYL establishes a unified evaluation framework combining full-field rollout errors, virtual probes, and drag and lift predictions to assess numerical accuracy and physical fidelity. To accompany the benchmark, we develop a Graph Transformer as a reference baseline predicting velocity and pressure fields autoregressively on unstructured meshes. Using ONECYL, we investigate geometric representations and physics-based regularization across the three Reynolds-number regimes. The results show that explicitly encoding the cylinder geometry through a level-set representation consistently improves long-horizon prediction accuracy and generalization to unseen geometries, while divergence-based regularization becomes increasingly beneficial as flow complexity increases. The ONECYL benchmark and its Graph Transformer baseline provide a reproducible framework for evaluating graph-based surrogate models and establish a foundation for future research on long-horizon prediction of unsteady bluff-body flows.

</details>


### [92] [Fish navigate a hydrodynamic maze via yaw-mediated lateral migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09024)
*Michael A. Calicchia,Rui Ni*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究设计了具有空间变化平均流速和湍流的水动力迷宫，发现鱼通过身体偏航（yaw）来逃离能量不利区域，利用升力实现横向迁移，并保持身体姿态接近但不超过失速条件以最大化升力收益而不显著增加阻力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解鱼类如何在复杂流动环境中感知和响应水动力线索，以能量高效的方式识别和利用有利流场条件，这是揭示鱼类运动策略的核心问题。

Method: 设计了具有空间变化的平均流和湍流的水动力迷宫，构建受控的异质流场景观，用于观察和量化鱼类的运动策略及身体姿态变化。

Result: 发现鱼类通过身体相对来流偏航来逃离能量不利区域，偏航姿态通过升力促进横向迁移并可能提高对局部流速变化的感知灵敏度；鱼类保持接近但低于预测失速条件的身体朝向，以在不大幅增加阻力的情况下利用升力。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了鱼类在异质水动力环境中导航的机制，对理解自然流场中的鱼类行为以及工程流动系统的设计具有潜在参考价值。

Abstract: In aquatic environments, gradients in flow velocity and turbulence define a constantly shifting landscape that sets the physical constraints on propulsion and energy expenditure in fish. Successfully navigating these complex flows depends on the ability to sense and respond to subtle hydrodynamic cues in an energy-efficient manner. Revealing these mechanisms is central to understanding how fish identify and exploit favorable flow conditions. To this end, we designed a hydrodynamic maze with spatially varying mean flow and turbulence, creating a controlled heterogeneous flow landscape for understanding fish movement strategies. We found that fish escape energetically unfavorable regions by yawing their bodies relative to the flow. These yawed orientations appear to facilitate lateral migration through lift forces and may improve sensitivity to local flow variations. Notably, fish maintain body orientations near, but below, predicted stall conditions to exploit lift without a significant increase in drag. These findings provide insight into how fish navigate heterogeneous hydrodynamic environments, with potential relevance for both natural and engineered flow systems.

</details>

### [93] [Preliminary design of continuous wave Low-Level RF systems for S3FEL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06407)
*Z. Y. Zhang,J. F. Zhu,H. L. Ding,J. W. Han,W. Li,J. Y. Yang,W. Q. Zhang*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文报告了深圳超导软X射线自由电子激光（S3FEL）中连续波低频射频（CW LLRF）系统的初步设计方案，涵盖了四种类型的LLRF系统：主加速器（超导1.3 GHz，168套）、谐波腔（超导3.9 GHz，16套）、VHF电子枪（常温216 MHz，4套）和聚束器（常温1.3 GHz，2套）系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S3FEL中CW LLRF系统承担调节腔体功率耦合、控制RF场幅度和相位以及维持腔体共振频率和相位等关键功能，确保电子束工作在加速相位上。由于不同类型加速腔对RF场和腔体控制的需求不同，需要针对性地设计四种LLRF系统。

Method: 根据S3FEL采用的加速腔类型（超导1.3 GHz、超导3.9 GHz、常温216 MHz、常温1.3 GHz），将CW LLRF系统划分为四类，每台固态放大器（SSA）配备专用的LLRF系统，并分别给出各类系统的初步设计方案。

Result: 完成了S3FEL中四种CW LLRF系统（主加速器168套、谐波腔16套、VHF电子枪4套、聚束器2套）的初步设计方案的制定。

Conclusion: 基于S3FEL不同加速腔类型和RF控制需求，提出了四类CW LLRF系统的初步设计框架，为后续详细设计与实现奠定基础。

Abstract: In the Shenzhen Superconducting Soft X-ray Free Electron Laser (S3FEL), Continuous Wave (CW) Low-Level Radio Frequency (LLRF) systems perform critical functions including adjusting the power coupling of accelerator cavities, regulating the amplitude and phase of the RF field, and maintaining the resonance frequency and phase of the cavities. These functions are essential to ensure the electron beam operates at the accelerating phase. Within S3FEL, each superconducting cavity is driven by a solid-state amplifier (SSA), with each SSA paired with a dedicated LLRF system. Based on the distinct acceleration cavities employed, the CW LLRF systems for S3FEL are categorized into four types: 1. Primary accelerator LLRF systems (superconducting, 1.3 GHz; quantity: 168), 2. Harmonic cavity LLRF systems (superconducting, 3.9 GHz; quantity: 16), 3. VHF electron gun LLRF systems (room temperature, 216 MHz; quantity: 4), 4. Buncher LLRF systems (room temperature, 1.3 GHz; quantity: 2). These four LLRF system categories exhibit differing requirements for RF field and acceleration cavity control. This report presents the preliminary design schemes for these four types of CW LLRF systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 系统地分析了超相对论带电粒子在晶体中由电子非相干散射引起的去沟道效应。考虑了粒子在凝聚态物质中电磁场的变化，同时涵盖collectivized电子和内壳层电子的激发，并纳入热振动和晶体原子非均匀周期分布的影响。将散射分为扩散过程和瞬时去沟道过程，推导了单位长度上平行/垂直于原子平面的均方投影散射角表达式。

Result: 获得了平行和垂直于原子平面的单位长度均方投影散射角表达式，这些表达式完全考虑了晶体电子对沿经典轨迹运动粒子的非局域动量转移效应，并可引入非相干散射的有效最小动量。

Conclusion: 所提出的表达式为模拟超相对论正电荷粒子的非相干散射提供了简单有效的方法，同时也论证了将散射分为扩散过程和瞬时去沟道过程的必要性。

Abstract: This article systematically examines the effects that must be taken into account when modeling the dechanneling of highly ultrarelativistic charged particles caused by incoherent scattering by electrons of crystal atoms. Changes in the particle electromagnetic field that occur in condensed matter at highly ultrarelativistic energies are taken into account. The excitation of both collectivized electrons and inner-shell electrons of atoms is considered, taking into account thermal vibrations and the inhomogeneous periodic distribution of atoms in crystals. Differences in the calculations of stopping power and mean square scattering angle by electron of channeled particles are described. The need to separate both of them into diffuse processes, accompanied by the transmission of a transverse momentum which does not exceed the transverse momentum of the channeled particles, and instantaneous dechanneling processes with a greater transmitted momentum, the variation range of which reaches several orders of magnitude, is substantiated. The main result of the present work is expressions for the mean square projected scattering angles per unit length in planes parallel and perpendicular to the atomic plane, which also allow for the introduction of effective minimum momenta for incoherent scattering. Expressions of both types take into consideration completely the nonlocal effects of momentum transfer from crystal electrons to particles moving along classical trajectories, and allow for the formulation of simple methods for modeling the incoherent scattering of ultrarelativistic positively charged particles.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者直接依据Hofmann的Vlasov-Poisson方程推导修正，无需拟合参数，识別出两处错误：缺失的(1∓2η̂²/α)因子和符号错误。通过比较1998年印刷版与修正版在稳定性图上的差异，量化了两者分歧的程度和分布，并评估了对PIP-II加速器工作点筛选的影响。

Result: 修正后的关系与作者后续专著中的相干调谐频移系数吻合，而印刷版分别偏差24%和127%。在S²≤10比较域内，印刷版与修正版在0.73-2.11%的单元上不一致，无方向偏差；在排除的单元中分歧达22%，印刷版在所有各向异性采样点都过度预测不稳定性。对PIP-II，修正图标记了32个可评估周期中的4个，包括一个三次奇分支上被二次筛选遗漏的点。

Conclusion: 1998年发表的高阶色散关系与支撑已发布图形计算之间存在不一致，修正后的关系更可靠。误差在远离典型工作点的区域集中可能解释了其长期未被发现的原因，但尚不能确定历史成因。修正对PIP-II等实际加速器的稳定性筛选有实质影响，且结果仍受两代码间关于横向发射度增长的5倍分歧影响。

Abstract: The Hofmann stability chart is used to screen working points in space-charge-dominated linacs. We identify two errors in its published higher-order dispersion relations: missing $(1\mp2\hatη^2/α)$ factors in the third-order $S^4$ coupling residues, and a sign error in the stated isotropic reduction of the fourth-order relation. Both corrections follow from Hofmann's Vlasov-Poisson equations without fitted parameters. They reproduce coherent tune-shift coefficients in the author's later monograph that the printed forms miss by 24% and 127%. Mode-resolved figures from a published application agree with the corrected relations and reject the printed forms, indicating an inconsistency between the 1998 equations and the calculations underlying those tested figures. We quantify the effect on the non-oscillatory stability chart. Inside the adopted $S^2\le10$ comparison domain, printed and corrected forms disagree on 0.73-2.11% of cells, with no preferred direction. Among excluded cells, disagreement reaches 22%, and the printed relation over-predicts instability at every sampled anisotropy. This concentration may help explain why the errors persisted, although it does not establish their historical cause. For PIP-II, the corrected chart flags four of thirty-two evaluable periods, including one on a third-order odd branch missed by a second-order screen. This count covers non-oscillatory modes only and remains conditional on an unresolved factor-five disagreement between two codes on transverse emittance growth.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将NSLS-II中两个现有的电磁偶极子替换为永磁complex bends (CBs)，对修改后的NSLS-II晶格进行线性光学设计、非线性优化以及机器误差评估，并通过刮板测量验证CB设计所需的束流孔径要求。

Result: 优化后的晶格提供了足够的动力学孔径和动量孔径，能够支持离轴注入和常规运行；该晶格可通过现有NSLS-II校正方案得到校正；刮板测量验证了CB安装位置附近的实际束流孔径满足设计需求。

Conclusion: 该研究为CB概念在存储环中的首次实验验证铺平了道路，证明CB晶格设计不仅理论上可行，而且在现有存储环中具备实际可操作性。

Abstract: The demand for brighter photon beams has driven the development of diffraction-limited storage rings with ultralow electron beam emittance. A complex bend (CB) lattice was recently proposed as an approach for the future NSLS-II upgrade. As a step toward this upgrade, NSLS-II plans to replace two existing electromagnetic dipoles with permanent-magnet CBs, providing the first experimental demonstration of the CB concept in a storage ring. This paper presents the linear optics design, nonlinear optimization, and machine error evaluation of the modified NSLS-II lattice. The optimized lattice provides sufficient dynamic and momentum apertures for off-axis injection and routine operation and can be corrected with the existing NSLS-II correction scheme. Scraper measurements near the planned CB installation location validate the beam aperture requirements used in the CB design.

</details>


### [97] [Flexible Spectral-Normalized Neural Gaussian Process for Dynamic Aperture Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08620)
*Yousra El-Bachir,Frederik Van der Veken,Davide di Croce,Carlo Emilio Montanari,Massimo Giovannozzi,Ekaterina Krymova,Tatiana Pieloni*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于大规模科学应用中可扩展不确定性量化的经验贝叶斯方法，用于自动调整异方差谱归一化神经高斯过程的超参数，将超参数学习直接集成到训练循环中以降低计算负担。方法在CERN大型强子对撞机的圆形粒子加速器动态孔径估计任务上得到验证，以更低计算成本实现竞争性的预测性能和良好校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模科学应用中，最先进的机器学习方法通常计算上不可行，难以实现可扩展的不确定性量化。传统方法（如大量粒子跟踪模拟）在时间与资源上代价过高，且手动调整超参数在许多实际场景中不切实际。

Method: 提出一个经验贝叶斯框架，用于自动调整异方差谱归一化神经高斯过程（SNGP）的超参数。该方法将超参数学习直接集成到训练循环中，既保留半贝叶斯神经模型的表达能力和不确定性感知特性，又显著降低计算负担。

Result: 在CERN大型强子对撞机的动态孔径估计任务上，该方法相比最先进方法以更低计算成本实现了有竞争力的预测性能和良好校准的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该经验贝叶斯框架为训练异方差神经模型提供了一种通用解决方案，适用于手动超参数调优不可行的场景，有望推广到其他面临类似计算限制的领域。

Abstract: We address the challenge of scalable uncertainty quantification in large-scale scientific applications, where complex state-of-the-art machine learning methods are often computationally infeasible. Our primary contribution is a simple yet effective empirical Bayes method for automatically tuning the hyperparameters of a flexible, heteroscedastic Spectral-normalized Neural Gaussian Process. This approach retains the expressiveness and uncertainty-awareness of semi-Bayesian neural models while significantly reducing the computational burden by integrating hyperparameter learning directly into the training loop. We demonstrate the practical impact of our method on the task of estimating the dynamic aperture in circular particle accelerators, a fundamental problem in high-energy physics colliders and storage rings, using simulation data from the case of the Large Hadron Collider at CERN. Traditional approaches to DA estimation require extensive particle-tracking simulations, which are prohibitively time-consuming and resource-intensive. Our results show that the proposed method achieves competitive predictive performance and well-calibrated uncertainty estimates at much lower computational cost than state-of-the-art approaches. We stress that, beyond this application, the proposed empirical Bayes framework offers a general solution for training heteroscedastic neural models in situations where manual hyperparameter tuning is impractical. Accordingly, we anticipate that this framework can be applied to other domains that encounter comparable computational limitations.

</details>

### [98] [Physical-Field Reconstruction from Sparse Observations: When Are Diffusion Models Preferable to Deterministic Regression?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06135)
*Hao Zhou,Rui Zhang,Qi Wang,Hao Sun*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文对确定性U-Net、条件扩散模型和先验引导扩散模型在稀疏物理场重建任务上进行了公平比较（涵盖2D Poisson方程、2D Navier-Stokes流和1D Kuramoto-Sivashinsky动力学），得出三个关键结论：重建精度依赖具体场域与参数区间且扩散模型无系统性优势；集成均值提升相位对齐精度而单个样本更保变异性与高波数能量；条件扩散在不确定性估计上更可靠且成本更低，先验引导扩散对掩码分布偏移更稳健但推理成本高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏观测下物理场重建本质上是不适定逆问题而非简单插值。尽管已有多种确定性与生成式方法，学界尚无共识判断何时点估计足够、何时需要分布级重建。作者旨在通过公平对照实验厘清生成式重建在不同场景下的适用边界。

Method: 在匹配的实验设置下（相同前后处理、相同数据、相同评价指标），对三类代表性方法进行系统比较：确定性U-Net、条件扩散模型（conditional diffusion）以及先验引导扩散（prior-guided diffusion），在2D Poisson方程、2D Navier-Stokes流和1D Kuramoto-Sivashinsky三个具有不同物理特性的基准上进行验证。

Result: 三项核心发现：(1) 各方法的重建精度取决于具体物理场与状态参数区间，扩散模型在更高复杂度或更稀疏观测下并无系统性优势；(2) 扩散模型的集成均值能改善相位对齐精度，而单个样本能更好地保留场变异性，在部分场景可维持高波数功率谱成分；(3) 条件扩散提供更可靠的不确定性估计且推理成本较低，先验引导扩散对掩码分布偏移（分布外掩码）更稳健，但推理代价显著更高且需精细的引导参数调节。

Conclusion: 该工作为稀疏物理场重建中何时应选择生成式方法提供了实用指导，并为不确定性估计的可靠性、细尺度样本保真度、分布偏移鲁棒性以及计算效率等维度的提升指明方向。生成式重建的价值应基于具体物理场特性与任务需求来判断，而非盲目追求更复杂的生成模型。

Abstract: Reconstructing physical fields from sparse observations is central to system identification, forecasting, and control, yet sparse measurements generally underdetermine the full field. This makes reconstruction an ill-posed inverse problem rather than simple interpolation. Although many deterministic and generative methods have been developed, there is still no clear consensus on when a single point estimate is sufficient and when a distribution of plausible reconstructions is more useful. We conduct a fair comparison of a deterministic U-Net, conditional diffusion, and prior-guided diffusion under matched experimental settings, including 2D Poisson equation, 2D Navier-Stokes flow, and 1D Kuramoto-Sivashinsky dynamics. Through this comparison, we make three observations. First, accuracy is field- and regime-dependent, with no systematic advantage for diffusion under higher complexity or sparser observations. Second, ensemble means improve phase-aligned accuracy, whereas individual samples better preserve variability and can retain high-wavenumber power in selected regimes. Third, conditional diffusion provides more reliable uncertainty estimates at lower cost, while prior-guided diffusion is more robust to mask-distribution shifts but requires substantially higher inference cost and guidance tuning. These results clarify when generative reconstruction is useful and provide guidance for improving uncertainty estimation, fine-scale sample fidelity, robustness, and computational efficiency in sparse field reconstruction.

</details>


### [99] [A Sparsity-Promoting Electromagnetic Inversion Method Regularized by $\ell_1 / \ell_2$-Norm of the Model Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06524)
*Lingqi Gao,Hakan Bagci*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以模型梯度ℓ₁/ℓ₂范数作为正则化项的非线性电磁（EM）反演方法。该方法利用ℓ₁/ℓ₂范数的尺度不变性更精确地表征解的稀疏性，通过交替方向乘子法（ADMM）将非凸非光滑的反演问题重构为包含五个子步骤的高效优化算法，其中第一步嵌入Gauss-Newton方法处理非线性。数值实验表明，该方法在介电常数重建中优于Tikhonov和全变差（TV）正则化，尤其在保持尖锐边缘和抑制伪影方面表现突出，且不增加计算成本，对噪声和正则化权重选择具有较强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的ℓ₁范数正则化在电磁反演中用于促进解的稀疏性，但其不具备尺度不变性，导致对稀疏性的表征不够准确。此外，现有正则化方法（如Tikhonov和TV）在保持尖锐边缘和抑制伪影方面存在不足，因此需要一种能够更精确刻画解的结构稀疏性且高效可解的正则化方法。

Method: 论文提出以模型梯度的ℓ₁/ℓ₂范数（即ℓ₁范数与ℓ₂范数之比）作为正则化项，该比值具有尺度不变性。为处理其引入的非凸性和非光滑性，将反演问题重构，并采用交替方向乘子法（ADMM）求解，得到包含五个子步骤的高效优化算法。其中第一个子步骤引入Gauss-Newton方法对非线性反演进行线性化处理。

Result: 通过合成数据和实验数据的多组数值算例验证，该方法在介电常数剖面重建中优于Tikhonov和全变差（TV）正则化方法，特别是在保持尖锐边缘和抑制伪影方面表现更优。同时，该方法不增加额外计算成本，对测量噪声表现出较强的鲁棒性，且对正则化权重的选择敏感性更低。

Conclusion: 基于ℓ₁/ℓ₂范数梯度正则化结合ADMM的非线性电磁反演方法，在重建质量、边缘保持、抗噪性能和参数鲁棒性等方面均优于传统正则化方法，是一种高效且实用的电磁反演技术。

Abstract: A nonlinear EM inversion method regularized by $\ell_1 / \ell_2$-norm of the model gradient is proposed. The $\ell_1 / \ell_2$-norm, defined as the ratio of $\ell_1$-norm to $\ell_2$-norm, exhibits a scale-invariant property that enables it to more accurately characterize sparsity in the solution compared with the conventional $\ell_1$-norm. To address the nonconvexity and nonsmoothness introduced by the quotient structure of $\ell_1 / \ell_2$-norm, the inversion problem is reformulated and solved using the alternating direction method of multipliers, resulting in an efficient optimization algorithm comprising five sub-steps. The Gauss--Newton method is incorporated into the first sub-step to linearize the nonlinear inversion. The proposed method is validated by a series of numerical examples using both synthetic and experimental datasets, and is compared against standard regularization methods such as Tikhonov and total variation. The results demonstrate that the proposed method yields superior reconstructions of permittivity profiles, particularly in preserving sharp edges and suppressing artifacts, without incurring additional computational cost. Furthermore, the method exhibits strong robustness to measurement noise and reduced sensitivity to the choice of the regularization weight.

</details>


### [100] [A HIP-Compatible Accelerator Backend for Fourier-Bessel Particle-in-Cell Simulations on CPU/DCU Heterogeneous Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06680)
*Jingliang Fan,Ruiqing He,Yang Wan,Jiandong Shang,Hengliang Guo,Qiang Chen*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文为FBPIC（傅里叶-贝塞尔粒子网格模拟代码）开发了兼容HIP的加速器后端，使其能在DCU平台上高效运行。在V100 GPU上对比原实现获得1.32-1.54倍加速，多DCU实验实现四加速器1.88倍强扩展加速和约68%弱扩展效率下2.72倍总吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FBPIC原始实现基于Numba CUDA后端，这一依赖限制了其在采用HIP编程环境的加速器（如国产DCU）上的直接部署。由于CUDA和HIP不直接互通，需要开发新的后端以支持FBPIC在DCU平台上高效运行，从而扩大其可部署的硬件生态。

Method: 开发了一个与HIP兼容的加速器后端，替代原有的Numba CUDA后端，使FBPIC能够在DCU平台上高效执行，同时保持Python用户接口和高级模拟工作流不变。通过通信分析识别可扩展性瓶颈。

Result: 在LWFA工作负载上，新后端在V100 GPU上比原FBPIC实现快1.32-1.54倍；在DCU平台上可实现高效执行。多DCU实验在四个加速器上获得1.88倍强扩展加速，弱扩展效率约68%时总吞吐量提升2.72倍。通信分析表明节点间通信和同步是主要可扩展性限制。

Conclusion: 该HIP兼容后端成功将FBPIC扩展到DCU平台并提升了性能。除FBPIC本身外，提出的移植方法为其他基于Python开发、面向异构加速器平台的科学计算应用提供了实用参考，并总结了DCU平台移植的关键经验教训。

Abstract: FBPIC (Fourier-Bessel particle-in-cell) is a high-performance simulation code for relativistic plasma and accelerator physics. Its original accelerator backend relies on Numba CUDA, which limits its direct deployment on accelerators using the HIP (Heterogeneous-Compute Interface for Portability) programming environment, such as DCU (Deep Computing Unit) accelerators. In this work, we develop an accelerator backend compatible with HIP that enables FBPIC to run efficiently on DCU platforms while preserving its Python user interface and high level simulation workflow. For the evaluated LWFA (laser-wakefield acceleration) workloads, the proposed backend achieves 1.32-1.54x speedups over the original FBPIC implementation on an NVIDIA V100 GPU and enables efficient execution on the DCU platform. We also summarize the key lessons learned from porting FBPIC to the DCU platform. Multi-DCU experiments achieve a 1.88x strong-scaling speedup on four accelerators and a 2.72x increase in aggregate throughput at approximately 68\% weak-scaling efficiency, with communication analysis identifying inter-node communication and synchronization as the main scalability limitations. Beyond FBPIC, the proposed approach provides a practical reference for porting and optimizing other scientific computing applications developed with Python on heterogeneous accelerator platforms.

</details>


### [101] [Beyond Residuals: Energy based solutions of partial differential equations using scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08239)
*Timon Rabczuk,Yizheng Wang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统性地重新审视深度能量方法（DEM），将其置于物理信息学习与变分建模的更广阔背景下，阐明了DEM与PINNs、VPINNs之间的关系，并通过增量变分公式扩展DEM以处理非线性、历史相关和时间相关问题（如相场断裂和耗散系统），最终通过数值算例论证DEM是残差类物理信息方法在变分问题上的有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差类物理信息神经网络（PINNs及其弱形式变体）通过损失最小化来强制方程的强形式或弱形式，需要残差加权并可能出现高阶导数计算问题。而基于能量的方法直接从变分原理出发，将解定义为能量的最小化者，具有更自然的物理一致性，但DEM尚未被系统性梳理和定位。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与数值实验相结合的方式：首先从变分原理出发，阐明DEM的理论基础与适用范围；其次系统对比DEM与PINNs、VPINNs在方法论层面的异同；然后着重引入增量变分公式，使DEM能够处理非线性、历史依赖和时间依赖问题，包括相场断裂与耗散系统；最后通过一系列数值算例验证DEM的有效性并分析其优缺点。

Result: 研究表明，能量最小化在稳定性、鲁棒性和可解释性方面对特定类别的问题具有内在优势；增量变分公式成功将DEM扩展至非线性和耗散系统；变分结构为优化和反问题提供了物理约束而非残差惩罚的自然基础。

Conclusion: DEM为变分问题提供了一种有原则且有效的替代残差类方法的新途径，在稳定性、鲁棒性和可解释性上具有优势，但也有其局限。该工作明确了DEM适用的问题类别，并为物理信息学习领域的模型选择提供了指导性框架。

Abstract: Energy-based approaches provide a natural and physically consistent framework for a large class of partial differential equations arising in solid and fluid mechanics, where the governing equations follow from variational principles. In contrast to residual-based physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) and their weak-form variants, which enforce the strong or weak form of the equations through loss minimization, the Deep Energy Method (DEM) directly computes the solution as the minimizer of an energy or incremental potential functional. This eliminates the need for residual weighting, avoids high-order derivatives, and enables the direct enforcement of physical constraints through the variational structure.In this work, we systematically revisit the Deep Energy Method, placing it in the broader context of physics-informed learning and variational modeling. We clarify the relationship between DEM, PINNs, and VPINNs, and identify the class of problems for which energy minimization provides intrinsic advantages in terms of stability, robustness and interpretability. Particular emphasis is placed on incremental variational formulations, which allow DEM to be applied to nonlinear, history-dependent and time-dependent problems, including phase-field fracture and dissipative systems. The variational structure underlying DEM further provides a natural foundation for optimization and inverse problems, where the energy functional acts as a physics-based constraint rather than a residual penalty. Through a series of numerical examples, we demonstrate that DEM offers a principled and effective alternative to residual-based methods for variational problems, highlighting its strengths and limitations relative to existing physics-informed approaches.

</details>


### [102] [A finite-strain logarithmic viscoelastic model for Antarctic ice shelves based on an additive split](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08451)
*Maxime Nutte,Sebastian Skatulla,Carlo Sansour*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于冰川冰的有限应变Maxwell本构模型，在对数应变空间中将Hencky应变分解为弹性和黏性部分，并在有限元框架中应用于理想化冰舌的裂缝前沿应力场分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰架主要通过崩解（calving）过程损失质量，而该过程受近前沿应力场控制，时间尺度跨越弹性弯曲与黏性蠕变。此前缺乏能够同时刻画弹性与黏性行为的有限应变模型来分析冰舌端部应力分布。

Method: 基于有限应变Maxwell模型，在对数应变空间（Hencky应变）中采用加性速率分裂为弹性（各向同性Hencky弹性）与黏性（Glen型幂律应变率）部分；采用中点评估与对偶张量的向后Euler校正进行时间积分，并在有限元框架中实现。

Result: 经过黏弹性柱体基准测试后，将模型应用于包含深度相关密度与模量、温度相关流动性以及带趾部或基底掏蚀悬崖几何的理想化冰舌，揭示了黏弹性与前沿形态对终部附近张力的控制作用。

Conclusion: 所提出的对数应变空间有限应变Maxwell黏弹性模型能有效模拟冰架前沿应力场，证明黏弹性效应与前沿形态共同主导终部张力分布。

Abstract: Ice shelves lose mass primarily by calving, a process controlled by the near-front stress field on timescales that span elastic flexure and viscous creep. We formulate a finite-strain Maxwell model for glacier ice in logarithmic strain space. The Hencky strain of a fixed reference configuration is split additively at the level of rates into elastic and viscous parts; the spring is isotropic Hencky elasticity and the dashpot is a Glen-type power law written on the logarithmic strain rate and its work-conjugate stress. At infinitesimal strain the dashpot coincides with Glen's flow law; the elastic strains in the ice-shelf configurations of this paper remain in that regime. The model is integrated with a midpoint evaluation and a backward-Euler correction of the trial dual, and implemented in a finite-element setting. After a viscoelastic column benchmark, the formulation is applied to an idealised ice tongue, including depth-dependent density and moduli, temperature-dependent fluidity, and cliff geometries with a frontal foot or basal undercutting. The resulting stress fields show how viscoelasticity and front morphology control tension near the terminus.

</details>


### [103] [Structure-Informed Bayesian Inference of Anomalous Transport and Hidden Molecular Trapping in Amorphous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08780)
*Andrey Ananev,Maria Potapova,Nikolay Kondratyuk,Timur Vostroknutov,Aleksey Khlyupin*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于离散Morse理论和贝叶斯正则化的新方法，用于从单粒子追踪轨迹中提取波动非晶及大分子介质中的局域捕获态，克服了传统几何算法在动态孔道边界下的系统性偏差，并在复杂角蛋白基质中的氢/甲烷输运研究中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波动非晶和大分子介质中的分子扩散控制着软物质物理、能量存储和生物膜等关键输运过程。从单粒子追踪轨迹中提取局域捕获态是基础性挑战：由于热致结构呼吸不断重构孔道边界，传统几何算法存在严重系统偏差，会在分子循环返回时错误合并不同的局域态，导致捕获参数估计失真。

Method: 本文从局部几何递归范式转向结构信息驱动的贝叶斯正则化方法。具体步骤包括：(1) 利用离散Morse理论从波动的宿主矩阵中提取时间不变的拓扑骨架；(2) 基于分子尺寸构建气体特异性的物理先验；(3) 通过两阶段概率精修过程对轨迹步长进行序列化分割，动态适应输运景观。算法整体为线性O(N)计算复杂度。

Result: 在合成粒子探索真实互连矩阵图的严苛基准环境中，该方法消除了系统性偏差，将宏观捕获参数偏差限制在百分之几以内，并保持最优线性O(N)计算缩放。将方法应用于I型角蛋白基质中氢气和甲烷的输运研究（代表高度曲折、柔性的大分子网络），成功解码了受限扩散的隐藏微观机制。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种可广泛适用的单粒子追踪分析策略，超越了传统几何方法的局限。开源代码和数据已公开，可即时推广到电池聚合物中的离子输运、细胞内蛋白质转运等多种追踪现象，具有广泛的跨学科影响潜力。

Abstract: Molecular diffusion in fluctuating amorphous and macromolecular media governs key transport processes across soft-matter physics, energy storage, and biological membranes. Extracting localized trapping states from single-particle tracking trajectories remains a fundamental challenge; because thermal structural breathing continuously reconfigures pore boundaries, conventional geometric algorithms suffer from severe systematic biases, erroneously merging distinct localized states during cyclic molecular returns. Here, we address this deadlock by shifting the paradigm from local geometric recurrence to a structure-informed Bayesian regularization. Leveraging discrete Morse theory, we extract the time-invariant topological skeleton of the fluctuating host matrix to construct robust, gas-specific physical priors that account for individual molecular dimensions. Trajectory steps are sequentially partitioned via a two-stage probabilistic refinement that dynamically adapts to the transport landscape. Benchmarked against a rigorous environment where synthetic particles explore the actual interconnected matrix graph, our approach eliminates systemic biases, restricting macroscopic trapping parameter deviations to just a few percent under optimal linear $O(N)$ computational scaling. Applied to hydrogen and methane transport within a type-I kerogen matrix, serving as a prototype for highly tortuous, flexible macromolecular networks, the method successfully decodes the hidden microscopic mechanisms of confined diffusion. To ensure immediate broad impact, the documented open-source code and data are made publicly available, offering an accessible strategy readily adaptable to a broad spectrum of tracking phenomena, from ion transport in battery polymers to protein trafficking within cellular environments.

</details>


### [104] [The Path Integral Monte Carlo Sign Problem Is Not Always NP-Hard: Harmonic Fermions Can Be Solved in Quadratic Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09071)
*Aarif Chaudhary,Jonas Valenzuela,Siu A. Chin*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明谐振子陷阱中的费米子PIMC模拟（含或不含配对谐振相互作用）的配分函数可从收缩行列式形式解析推导，并可在λ-环语言下重整为闭合形式的有限珠子配分函数，通过O(n²)算法精确评估，完全绕过符号问题的数值不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子路径积分蒙特卡洛（PIMC）中的符号问题一直是量子多体模拟的核心障碍，普遍认为所有真正的费米子符号问题都是NP-hard的。作者试图寻找一个具体的框架来反驳这一观点，证明在某些特定但非平凡的系统（谐波陷阱中的费米子）中，符号问题可以被完全绕过。

Method: 从传播子的收缩行列式形式出发，解析推导任意离散虚时间片（珠子）数量的配分函数；将该配分函数重新表述为λ-环语言，利用置换统计导出二维情况下的闭合形式有限珠子配分函数；设计了一种特殊的O(n²)时间复杂度的数值算法来精确评估该闭合形式配分函数。

Result: 该方法可精确且数值稳定地再现原始（不可执行的）费米子PIMC模拟的能量，适用于n=10^4甚至更多费米子的系统，完全绕过了符号问题的数值不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作为费米子PIMC符号问题是否必然NP-hard提供了具体反例，表明在谐波陷阱等特定系统中符号问题可被完全规避，这一发现对量子蒙特卡洛领域的广泛预期构成了重要修正。

Abstract: In the Path Integral Monte Carlo (PIMC) simulation of fermions in a harmonic trap, with and without pairwise harmonic interactions, the partition functions for any discrete number of imaginary time slices (or beads) and for any choice of the short-time propagator can be analytically obtained from the contracted determinant form of the propagator. This work shows that the resulting recursion relation can be reformulated in the $λ$-ring language, yielding a closed-form finite-bead partition function in two dimensions in terms of permutation statistics. This closed-form partition function can be evaluated by a special algorithm in $O(n^2)$ time, providing an exact and numerically stable scheme for reproducing the energies of the original (undoable) fermion PIMC simulation for $n=10^4$ or more fermions. This result provides a concrete framework in which the numerical instability of the sign problem is completely bypassed, and serves as a counterexample to the prevailing view that all truly fermionic PIMC sign problems are NP-hard.

</details>
